42:     # --- SHIFT CONFIGURATION ---

45:

59:

61:     hold_status = models.CharField(max_length=10, choices=HOLD_STATUS_CHOICES, default='none', null=True, blank=True)
62:     hold_request_date = models.DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)
63:     hold_request_duration = models.CharField(max_length=20, null=True, blank=True)
64: 
65:     # --- LOCKING ---
66:     is_locked = models.BooleanField(default=False, help_text="If True, seat is locked by librarian and cannot be occupied")
67:     locked_shifts = models.CharField(max_length=50, blank=True, null=True, default='', help_text="Comma-separated list of locked shifts: morning, evening, full")

72: 
73:     def clean(self):
74:         super().clean()
75: 
76:         # Shift-based seats are strictly Ground Floor seats 40–53
77:         if self.is_shift_enabled:
78:             try:
79:                 seat_no = int(self.seat_number)
80:             except ValueError:
81:                 raise ValidationError("Seat number must be numeric for shift-enabled seats.")
82: 
83:             if self.floor != 'Ground Floor' or not (40 <= seat_no <= 53):
84:                 raise ValidationError(
85:                     "Shift-based seating is allowed only for Ground Floor seats 40–53."
86:                 )
87:             
88:     def mark_available(self):
89:         self.hold_student = None
90:         self.status = 'available'
91:         self.hold_status = 'none'
92:         self.hold_start_date = None
93:         self.available_since = timezone.now()
94:         self.save()
95: 
96:     def recalc_status(self, save=True):
97:         """
98:         Derives Seat.status from its active SeatAssignments.
99:         Rule:
100:         - No active assignments -> available
101:         - All active assignments on hold -> on_hold
102:         - Any active assignment NOT on hold -> occupied
103:         """
104:         active = self.assignments.filter(is_active=True)
105:         if not active.exists():
106:             # Check for GENUINE pending requests (not historical records)
107:             has_pending = self.assignments.filter(
108:                 is_active=False, 
109:                 student__status='pending', 
110:                 student__seat_id=self.id
111:             ).exists()
112:             
113:             if has_pending:
114:                 self.status = 'pending'
115:             else:
116:                 self.status = 'available'
117:         else:
118:             # If ANY active assignment is NOT on hold, it's occupied.
119:             # Only if ALL are on hold do we show on_hold color globally.
120:             if any(a.hold_status != 'active' for a in active):
121:                 self.status = 'occupied'
122:             else:
123:                 self.status = 'on_hold'
124:         
125:         if save:
126:             self.save(update_fields=['status'])
127:         return self.status
128: 
129: 
130:     def is_hold_expired(self, today=None):
131:         """
132:         Detects expired hold.
133:         DOES NOT modify seat or assignments.
134:         """
135:         if self.status != 'on_hold':
136:             return False
137: 
138:         if not self.hold_end_date:
139:             return False
140: 
141:         today = today or timezone.localtime(timezone.now()).date()
142:         return today > self.hold_end_date
143:

151:         return f"{self.floor} - Seat {self.seat_number} ({self.status})"

13705:     when locked out upon dashboard access.

13723:             return JsonResponse({'status': 'error', 'message': 'Student profile not found.'}, status=404)

13727:             email = email.lower().strip()

13729:             if not re.match(r'^[\w\.-]+@[\w\.-]+\.\w+$', email):

13733:             if DjangoUser.objects.filter(email__iexact=email).exclude(pk=user.pk).exists() or \

13735:                StudentAchievement.objects.filter(email__iexact=email).exclude(user=user).exists():
13736:                 return JsonResponse({'status': 'error', 'message': 'This email address is already registered in the system.'}, status=400)
13737: 
13738:         # Validate Whatsapp if provided
13739:         if whatsapp:
13740:             whatsapp = whatsapp.strip()
13741:             if not whatsapp.isdigit() or len(whatsapp) != 10:
13742:                 return JsonResponse({'status': 'error', 'message': 'WhatsApp number must be exactly 10 digits.'}, status=400)
13743:             
13744:             # Check uniqueness of whatsapp number across StudentProfile and StudentAchievement

13746:                StudentAchievement.objects.filter(models.Q(whatsapp_number=whatsapp) | models.Q(mobile_number=whatsapp)).exclude(user=user).exists():

185: def send_admission_approval_notifications(student, seat=None):
186:     """
187:     Sends admission approval notifications asynchronously in a background thread.
188:     Accepts an optional 'seat' object.
189:     """
190:     import threading
191:     
192:     def _send_notifs():
193:         try:
194:             # Get the service details (which will include the seat info if it exists)
195:             service_details = get_student_service_details(student)
196:             
197:             if student.user.email:
198:                 send_approval_email(student, seat, service_details)
199:             
200:             # Send WhatsApp regardless of email presence
201:             send_approval_whatsapp(student, service_details)
202:         except Exception as e:
203:             logger.error(f"Error in send_admission_approval_notifications thread: {e}")
204: 
205:     thread = threading.Thread(target=_send_notifs)
206:     thread.daemon = True
207:     thread.start()
208:

211:     """

215:     from .utils import get_user_notification_email

231:             fail_silently=False,
232:         )
233: 
234:         logger.info(f"Approval email sent to {student.full_name} ({target_email})")
235: 
236:     except Exception as e:
237:         logger.error(f"Failed to send approval email: {e}")

1278: def process_birthday_wishes():
1279:     """
1280:     Daily background processor: Checks for students/alumni whose DOB matches today.
1281:     Dispatches In-App notifications and celebratory HTML Emails.
1282:     """
1283:     from .models import StudentProfile, Notification
1284:     from .email_service import send_html_email
1285: 
1286:     today = timezone.localtime(timezone.now()).date()
1287:     print(f"--- Running Daily Birthday Wish Check for {today} ---")
1288: 
1289:     birthday_students = StudentProfile.objects.filter(
1290:         dob__month=today.month,
1291:         dob__day=today.day
1292:     ).select_related('user')
1293: 
1294:     sent_count = 0
1295:     for student in birthday_students:
1296:         cooldown_key = f"birthday_{student.id}_{today.year}"
1297:         already_sent = Notification.objects.filter(
1298:             user=student.user,
1299:             category="general",
1300:             created_at__date=today,
1301:             meta__cooldown_key=cooldown_key
1302:         ).exists()
1303: 
1304:         if not already_sent:
1305:             create_notification(
1306:                 user=student.user,
1307:                 title=f"🎂 Happy Birthday, {student.full_name}! 🎉",
1308:                 message=f"Team ABCD wishes you a very Happy Birthday! May your day be filled with joy and your year with grand success! 🎈🎁",
1309:                 link=f"{settings.SITE_URL}{reverse('users:student_dashboard')}",
1310:                 category="general",
1311:                 meta={"cooldown_key": cooldown_key}
1312:             )
1313: 
1314:             student_email = get_user_notification_email(student)
1315:             if student_email:
1316:                 try:
1317:                     send_html_email(
1318:                         subject=f"🎂 Happy Birthday from Team ABCD, {student.full_name}! 🎉",
1319:                         to_email=student_email,
1320:                         template="emails/birthday_wish_email.html",
1321:                         context={
1322:                             "student": student,
1323:                             "dashboard_url": f"{settings.SITE_URL}{reverse('users:student_dashboard')}"
1324:                         },
1325:                         fail_silently=True
1326:                     )
1327:                 except Exception as e:
1328:                     print(f"Birthday email failed for {student.full_name}: {e}")
1329: 
1330:             sent_count += 1
1331:             print(f" > Sent Birthday Wish to {student.full_name}")
1332: 
1333:     print(f"--- Birthday Check Complete. Sent {sent_count} wishes. ---")
1334:     return sent_count
